# Native engine parity ledger

The parity ledger required by `llm-docs/pipkin-rust-engine-plan.md` §1 step 5 and §2. Every
row is one baseline capability that the native engine must preserve (or a recorded reason it is
not part of the baseline). **Nothing in this ledger is "passed".** A row only passes at
qualification, with a differential assertion against the frozen reference plus native evidence.
Rows cite the native tests that exercise them and say whether any comparison with
reference-derived output exists.

- **Reference baseline:** upstream pin `d2a311097cbcf669e699479587332ae3988a49d0` staged by
  `scripts/build-engine.sh` with `packaging/pi-onboarding.patch` + `packaging/pi-onboarding/*.ts`
  (bridge sha256 `370dbef417d1cdeb68239f0d283d4dceb36f5ed539e500e0ba041238aad94e3a`) and
  `packaging/pi-subagents.patch` + `packaging/pi-subagents/*.ts` (subagents sha256
  `9fb846e55a931d829e2ca25bde08ae98d2f128c14ceb6f1bc033b0a2e6fb3664`), model data manifest
  `b92d631bb8bb2cac6f824a03a814baad9452164c518426b8bc4bbc5c2142bb05` (archive
  `2be5f68d90695e71372c01ccbd08d5728b93691e07c45918846997c8949bcabb`), plus the Pipkin desktop
  at app base `75f5c35`. Frozen reference fixtures: `fixtures/pi-sessions/` (8 scenarios, RE-02).
- **Current follow-up:** actual 1,500-turn desktop and uninterrupted 720-turn mixed
  soaks on `9d61b26`, protocol 12/schema 10 unchanged. All replies and 1,020 combined
  real tool effects/durable outputs verify; both desktop/server exits are clean. Desktop
  server peak **131,140 KiB** exceeds the 100 MiB reference target for this expanded
  single-active-turn workload; desktop residual growth remains open. The uninterrupted
  two-active-turn worker peaks at 101,976 KiB with 13 idle fds and zero settled descendants.
  See [sustained desktop evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/desktop-soak.md). Counts
  and full-qualification status stay unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** executed queue comparison following `e6951d4`, protocol
  12/schema 10 unchanged. Eleven actual Pi/native runs compare queue boundaries, model
  requests, visible history and per-input answers. Failed queues now wait for explicit input,
  including after reopen; new idle admissions preserve older queue order and one/all modes.
  Capacity/idempotency and real SIGKILL settlement-boundary checks cover recovery. See
  [queue evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/queue-runtime.md). Aggregate evidence counts
  and full-qualification status are unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** executed generation comparison following `9fd1bb5`, protocol
  12/schema 10 unchanged. Twenty-five Pi/native cases compare full projected traces, history,
  outcomes, compaction and native restart/replay. Fixed final stop/length tool execution,
  overflow taking ordinary retries, and unsolicited provider-abort retries. Forty-eight
  classifiers and seven delay cases agree. See [generation evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/generation-control-flow.md).
  GEN-09 now distinguishes SDK helper detection from actual durable generation behavior;
  sequential-setting reachability is audited. Evidence counts and full-qualification status
  are unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** built-in orphan/fault recovery following `2369416`, protocol
  12/schema 10 unchanged. Actual Pi newer-task refusal/abort and faulted-generation cleanup
  are captured as SQLite fixtures; native import preserves outcomes, partial history and
  next-request context, then continues real tools across restart without repeated effects.
  Failed orphan-child Stop persistence now cancels execution and propagates failure before
  parent settlement. See [recovery evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/orphan-recovery.md).
  `REC-ORPHAN` advances not started → measured for this bounded scope: 445 measured,
  9 observed, 1 unverified, 49 not started; full qualification remains open.
- **Prior follow-up:** original zune-inflate 0.2.54 notices following `e0c0ab5`.
  All 14 original package files and two recovered notices match the recorded upstream tree;
  inventory records its declared Zlib alternative. Actual native Arch notice hashes and
  installed coding/migration/replay pass. Missing-notice entries decrease five → four;
  614 Rust packages and 33 applicability/review flags remain. Other gaps are audited explicitly
  in [notice recovery evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/notice-source-recovery.md).
  Ledger counts and qualification status are unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** subagent storage-failure recovery following `2aea0e8`, protocol
  12/schema 10 unchanged. Read-only and admission-only parent faults cancel real child tools;
  streamed child storage failure also fails the parent closed. Explicit parent reopen
  reconciles the existing child, preserving one result and never repeating unsafe effects.
  See [recovery evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/subagent-storage-recovery.md). Counts
  are unchanged; corrected the stale evidence-summary cell from 51 to 50 not-started rows
  to match the 504 actual rows and existing totals. Native-default qualification remains open.
- **Prior follow-up:** packaged desktop keyboard focus, Stop and crash recovery following
  `9915177`, protocol 12/schema 10 unchanged. Actual desktop testing reproduced the empty
  workspace focus failure; a GPUI regression and guarded packaged workflow verify the fix.
  Real write tools/diffs, streaming, Stop, next turn, draft/history recovery and owned-process
  cleanup pass; same-package crash/reopen preserves exact durable rows and six provider
  requests. See [desktop evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/packaged-desktop.md). Existing
  evidence counts and full-qualification status stay unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** actual native Arch packaging following `c64e59b`, protocol
  12/schema 10 unchanged. `PIPKIN_ENGINE=native makepkg` no longer requires Pi/npm, and
  native mode preserves Cargo release line tables without makepkg's full-debug override.
  The actual artifact passes metadata/recipe/binary/1,079-notice-hash checks and isolated
  import/coding/restart/archive restoration with Node/Pi traps unused. See
  [Arch evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/native-arch-package.md). `PKG-PKGBUILD`
  advances not started → observed; full qualification and native-default remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** distinct durable tool identities and matched packaged performance
  following `42b196d`, protocol 12/schema 10. Reused provider IDs now execute independently
  while provider context retains originals; restart/replay/interrupted-call tests and actual
  schema-9 → schema-10 upgrade/backup/refusal pass. Actual Pi/native rollback passes on the
  new artifacts. See [identity evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/tool-call-identities.md).
  The final 20-pair/six-turn workload passes all 240 effects and resource gates: native/Pi
  p95 readiness 42.984/830.414 ms, idle RSS 32,048/464,284 KiB, peak 37,708/494,304 KiB.
  See [paired evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/paired-performance.md) for scope and limits.
  Counts and full-qualification status stay unchanged; native remains opt-in.
- **Prior follow-up:** published notice filename discovery following `3fa2d98`;
  see [notice evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/notice-filenames.md). The inventory now
  retains yaml-rust2's existing MIT/Apache-prefixed notices byte for byte. Native missing
  notices decrease from six to five, review flags from 34 to 33. Other license/provenance
  review and release gates remain open; earlier package measurements are unchanged.
- **Prior follow-up:** actual installed Pi/native rollback and matched package sizes
  following `d112c77`; see [cross-engine evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/installed-pi-rollback.md).
  Headless migration, native continuation, complete desktop/native archive restore, Pi
  ownership refusal, explicit release and resumed Pi coding pass against actual packages.
  Matched binaries yield 44.17% installed / 50.69% archive reductions, meeting the package
  targets. Desktop acceptance, CI execution and remaining release gates stay open.
- **Prior follow-up:** installed native upgrade/rollback and archive restore following
  `cf32a31`; see [installed evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/installed-upgrade-restore.md).
  Two real native packages continue coding across upgrade, rollback and complete native
  data/config restoration. Full retained output survives temporary spill deletion; newer
  schemas are refused unchanged. Rollback now rejects damaged or unresponsive packages
  before activation. Real Pi rollback and desktop/profile acceptance remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** native model-pattern and locale matching following `52b60c7`;
  see [model-scope evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/model-scope.md). All 416 captured
  reference cases pass, plus runtime model dispatch/live settings/restart and locale
  isolation checks. Native packaging and required workspace checks pass. Extreme pattern
  bounds, broader locales and release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** sustained mixed workload on `ca5dd74`; see
  [mixed-workload evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/mixed-workload.md). Two 720-turn
  runs pass with eight sessions, two concurrent turns, real 1 MiB tool output, 64 KiB
  streaming replies and three orderly restarts. Final idle/peak RSS 30,444/99,612 KiB,
  13 idle fds throughout, all 720 durable tool outputs byte-exact and effects unique.
  Retained output/history explains database growth; positive RSS slopes and broader
  desktop/uninterrupted/release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** final-response ownership following `368fdca`; see
  [large-output memory evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/owned-final-message.md).
  Moving the response through its commit/publication removes two full copies. All 27 streamed
  outputs remain exact; worst 10 MiB peak including reopen is 98,112 KiB, below 100 MiB.
  The full text soak and stream latency pass; broader sustained/desktop qualification is open.
- **Prior follow-up:** six release performance benchmarks on `4a9d4eb`; see
  [qualification performance evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/qualification-performance.md).
  The full 1500-turn text soak passes its memory/fd/process budgets, all 27 streamed lengths
  through 10 MiB are exact, and current startup/stream/Stop/page latency is measured. The 10 MiB
  response peaks at 115.72 MiB; desktop/multi-session/long-tool and relative Pi gates remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** native-only history labels following `ee5b5d7`, protocol 12 / schema 9;
  see [history provenance evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/native-history-labels.md).
  Snapshots/pages expose the saved import boundary; native rows, child previews and offline copies
  distinguish later native work without changing source content. Visual/installed rollback acceptance
  and full archive/restore qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** HTTP timeout validation following `2eec3e1`; see
  [timeout evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/http-timeout-validation.md).
  Invalid supplied timeouts now refuse affected generation, login and transport operations;
  31 executed reference getter/parser cases cover values, errors and precedence. Native stays
  available for live repair rather than failing startup; broader timeout qualification is open.
- **Prior follow-up:** compaction validation following `cf5043a`; see
  [settings evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/compaction-settings-validation.md).
  Invalid ordinary/selected-model tokens now fail on use while unrelated settings and
  other models remain intact. Forty-six executed reference settings cases and runtime
  live-repair/compaction tests cover this change; broader settings qualification stays open.
- **Prior follow-up:** explicit native import source following `6b148ee`; see
  [source flag evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/import-source-flag.md).
  `--pi-agent-dir` selects all native import operations without changing native storage or
  importing at startup. Headless tests cover explicit-source discovery, confirmed session/
  config/credential imports, preserved source bytes and continuation of imported history.
- **Prior follow-up:** invalid-proxy preflight following `367e389`; see
  [validation evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/proxy-validation.md). Shared HTTP rejects
  invalid configuration before I/O, including login, catalog, redirects and pooled requests.
  Eight executed reference resolver cases agree on routing and unsupported protocols; native
  malformed-URL errors omit credential-bearing raw URLs. Broader qualification remains open.
- **Prior follow-up:** plain HTTP CONNECT following `bdc7a26`; see
  [tunnel evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/http-connect-tunnels.md). A verified, opt-in
  reqwest patch retains the existing transport and matches eight pinned-undici origin/proxy
  combinations. Pool reuse, cancellation, credential separation and native runtime routing are
  measured; the other proxy diagnostics, retries and release gates remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** configured extra CA trust following `d948887`; see
  [trust evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/extra-ca-trust.md). Ten executed Node/undici
  TLS cases cover extra roots and malformed bundles. Native HTTP, credential clients and
  WebSockets share configured trust; changed trust/identity/destination invalidates pooled
  WebSockets. Live bundle reload is a native advantage; broader network qualification remains open.
- **Prior follow-up:** AWS credential isolation following `0c3d742`; see
  [isolation evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/aws-credential-isolation.md). Cache identities
  include source configuration and credentials; isolated Bedrock helpers use request environment,
  bounded output and owned process cleanup. Twelve executed SDK helper cases are compared locally.
  Live AWS, credential retries and release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** Google certificate federation following `d1809e7`; see
  [certificate evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/google-certificate.md). Nineteen executed
  SDK cases use real local mutual TLS; native checks cover proxy/idle settings, identity rotation,
  source impersonation and separation from model requests. Google idle cache eviction is now LRU.
  Broader credential/release qualification remains open.
- **Prior follow-up:** Google AWS federation following `d0e949b`; see
  [AWS federation evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/google-aws.md). Thirteen SDK cases
  cover environment/metadata discovery, IMDSv2, rotating roles and exact signed proofs.
  Certificate sources and broader credential/release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** executable Google ADC helpers following `d721b3a`; see
  [helper evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/google-executable.md). Thirteen executed
  SDK outcomes, bounded process cleanup, configured timeouts and Vertex streams are measured.
  AWS/certificate sources and broader credential/release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** Google file/URL federation and external-user refresh following
  `28b0e4d`; see [external ADC evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/google-external.md).
  Eight executed SDK cases and actual Vertex streams pass. AWS/executable/certificate
  sources and broader credential/release qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** Google ADC metadata and impersonation following `8abc540`;
  see [ADC evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/google-adc.md). Token caches now distinguish
  replacement credential contents and refresh independently. External-account federation
  and full cloud/provider qualification remain open.
- **Prior follow-up:** native AWS `login_session` credentials following `fe4a15a`;
  see [login evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/aws-login.md). Executed SDK outcomes,
  cryptographically verified DPoP proofs, cancellation and signed Bedrock requests pass.
  Live AWS acceptance and credential retry middleware are not inferred from these tests.
- **Prior follow-up:** native AWS IAM Identity Center credentials following `8a0ac3d`;
  see [SSO evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/aws-sso.md). Legacy/session caches, refresh,
  cancellation, bounded responses and signed Bedrock requests pass. MFA callback refusal
  matches Pi; the separate AWS `login_session` gap identified here is closed by the follow-up above.
- **Prior follow-up:** fork history migration and installed coding checks on top of `c508954`;
  see [fork/import evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/fork-import.md). Executed Pi SQLite
  contexts match native imports; installed write/edit/bash/read, restart and durable replay
  pass without Node/Pi. Native CI is wired but its runner execution is unverified.
- **Prior follow-up:** Anthropic workload identity federation on top of `d211c03`; see
  [federation evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/anthropic-federation.md). Local exchanges,
  refresh/rotation/cancellation, native restart and executed SDK comparisons pass. Counts
  are unchanged; prior refusal-only measurement did not establish working federation.
- **Prior follow-up:** Copilot login retry bounds and subagent usage/capacity on top of
  `4a0601f`; see [retry evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/copilot-login-retries.md) and
  [subagent evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/subagent-accounting.md).
- **Prior follow-up:** authenticated Radius catalogs on top of `73979ae`; see
  [Radius catalog evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/radius-catalog.md). Private catalog
  discovery, independent persistence, offline migration and a real native tool/restart turn
  are implemented and tested; PRV-radius returns PA → NC.
- **Prior follow-up:** credential refresh and cancellation on top of `1429251`; see
  [refresh evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/provider-refresh.md). Two refresh rows now have
  measured evidence; PRV-radius reopens for missing dynamic catalog behavior.
- **Prior follow-up:** product prompt/help, provider identity and OAuth reachability on top of
  `291e854` (2026-10-11), protocol 11 / schema 9; see [identity/auth evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/identity-auth-audit.md).
  Prior durable root/child removal: [removal evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/session-removal.md).
  Prior model/thinking configuration: [cycle evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/model-cycle.md).
  Prior prompt-resource fix: [resource/provider evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/prompt-resource-provider-audit.md).
  Catalog refresh/offline implementation: [catalog evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/remote-catalog.md).
- **Historical broad code reconciliation:** `e4c3a8c` (2026-10-11), including the Claude lifecycle
  checks; see [reconciliation](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/parity-reconciliation.md). The following
  revision describes the broader historical audit; retained measurements keep their identities.
- **Native revision cited:** branch `native-engine` at `4fc4060` (engine protocol 2, engine
  schema 1). Rows were re-inventoried from the merged code and its tests (file + function names
  verified by script). "measured" means a non-ignored automated test that passed in the latest
  recorded workspace runs (progress record §6: 1171 passed / 0 failed / 53 ignored at the handoff
  commit; 1179 / 0 / 53 on the packaging branch after merging `ae95240`); the final check at
  `4fc4060` is recorded by the lead in the progress record ("Handoff verification"). The 8

  reference-corpus tool tests (`trd::matches_a_reference_corpus`, `ted::matches_a_reference_corpus`)
  are `#[ignore]` with no recorded run and are never counted.
- **Audit sources:** `llm-docs/pipkin-rust-engine-audit/` (model-produced from source reading;
  a claim there is a lead for a differential test, not evidence).
- **ADRs:** `llm-docs/pipkin-rust-engine-adr/0001`–`0007`. **Handoff (Claude

## Current owner scope: extension compatibility excluded

On 2026-10-11 the user explicitly removed Pi extensions from Pipkin's requirements. See the
[plan scope amendment](pipkin-rust-engine-plan.md#owner-scope-change--2026-10-11-pi-extensions-are-not-required).
`OS` rows preserve their baseline and implementation evidence, but are excluded from release
gates. Existing native extension code is optional. No TypeScript loader, package ports, remote
extension services or reload work is needed for cutover. Built-in tools/providers/subagents
and general history/data preservation remain required. Older blocked-extension statements
below describe the historical requirement and are superseded by this decision.

## Legend

Path shorthand (reference, relative to `<pi-staged>/packages/`): `ai/` = `ai/src/`, `ca/` =
`coding-agent/src/`, `ce/` = `ca/experimental/`, `svc/` = `ce/services/`, `D/` = `durable/src/`,
`chord/` = `chord/src/`. Pipkin: `E/` = `crates/pipkin-engine/src/`, `P/` =
`crates/pipkin-engine-protocol/src/`, `App/` = `crates/pipkin-app/src/`, `Core/` =
`crates/pipkin-core/src/`, `pkg/` = `packaging/`. Audit files: `A-tools` =
`engine-tools-durable-usage.md`, `A-prov` = `providers-auth.md`, `A-svc` = `service-api.md`,
`A-ext` = `extensions-subagents.md`, `A-prompt` = `prompt-settings-layout.md`.

Test citations are `<prefix>::<function>`; a prefix names one file:

| Area | Prefixes |
| --- | --- |
| Engine core | `subacct::` `E/runtime/subagent_accounting_tests.rs`; `st::` `E/store/tests.rs`; `rt::` `E/runtime/tests.rs`; `rts::` `E/runtime/settings_tests.rs`; `rtf::` `E/runtime/fault_tests.rs` (storage faults, subagent recovery, retry); `rti::` `E/runtime/import_tests.rs`; `cfg::` `E/config.rs`; `wp::` `crates/pipkin-engine/tests/worker_process.rs` (real headless worker); `cp::` `crates/pipkin-engine/tests/crash_points.rs` (real headless worker, SIGKILL at each durable boundary, local HTTP stub; Linux); `pxi::` `crates/pipkin-engine/tests/proxy_isolation.rs` (own binary: sets proxy variables in the real process environment); `perf::` `crates/pipkin-engine/tests/perf.rs` (ignored benchmarks) |
| External attachment | `ep::` `E/endpoint/unix/tests.rs` (real local sockets, scripted providers) |
| Provider progress | `pg::` `E/runtime/run/progress.rs` (bounded checkpoints, stale callbacks, failures) |
| Session lifecycle | `life::` `E/runtime/lifecycle/tests.rs` (attachments, retirement, disconnect, extension generations) |
| Protocol | `frm::` `P/frame.rs`; `cnt::` `P/content.rs`; `dq::` `P/delivery.rs`; `delivery::` `E/delivery.rs` |
| Providers | `err::` `E/provider/errors.rs`; `mdl::` `E/provider/model.rs`; `sse::` `E/provider/sse.rs`; `phttp::` `E/provider/http.rs`; `prx::` `E/provider/proxy.rs`; `com/<file>::` `E/provider/api/common/<file>.rs`; `anth::` anthropic_messages; `oaic::` openai_completions; `oair::` openai_responses; `oairs::` openai_responses_shared; `azr::` azure_openai_responses; `cdx::` `E/provider/api/openai_codex/tests.rs` (`cdx::golden::<name>` = goldens produced by running the reference `stream()`); `gga::` google_generative_ai; `gsh::` google_shared; `gvx::` google_vertex; `gau::` google_auth; `gadc::` google_adc_tests; `gext::` google_external_tests; `gexe::` google_executable_tests; `gaws::` google_aws_tests; `gcert::` google_certificate_tests; `mis::` mistral_conversations; `brk::` bedrock_converse_stream; `bes::` bedrock_eventstream; `aws::` aws_auth; `sso::` aws_sso_tests; `awslogin::` aws_login_tests; `pim::` pi_messages; `cf::` cloudflare (all under `E/provider/api/`) |
| Auth | `ghretry::` `E/auth/oauth/github_copilot/retry_tests.rs`; `radiuscat::` `E/auth/remote_catalog/radius/tests.rs`; `arefresh::` `E/auth/refresh_tests.rs`; `auth::` `E/auth/tests.rs`; `astore::` `E/auth/store.rs`; `aprov::` `E/auth/providers.rs`; `mjson::` `E/auth/models_json.rs`; `cval::` `E/auth/config_value.rs`; `oauth::` `E/auth/oauth/tests.rs`; `pkce::`, `cb::` (callback), `dev::` (device), `gh::` (github_copilot), `cdx-oauth::` (openai_codex) in `E/auth/oauth/` |

| Desktop (backend-independent) | `inst::` `App/install.rs`; `diag::` `App/diagnostics.rs`; `ctl::` `App/controller.rs`; `stor::` `App/storage.rs`; `core-own::` `crates/pipkin-core/tests/ownership.rs`; `ui-cmd::` `crates/pipkin-ui/src/shell/commands.rs`; `pit::` `App/adapters/pi/tests.rs` (the Pi adapter against the mock Pi server); `cache::` `App/cache.rs`; `launch::` `App/launch.rs`; `demo::` `App/adapters/demo.rs`; `core-sub::` `crates/pipkin-core/tests/subagents.rs`; `core-imp::` `Core/import.rs`; `ui-sub::` `crates/pipkin-ui/src/shell/subagents/tests.rs`; `ui-onb::` `crates/pipkin-ui/src/shell/onboarding.rs` |
| Pi reference (not native evidence) | `e2e::` `App/adapters/pi/e2e.rs`; `rpi::` `App/adapters/pi/real_pi.rs` (ignored unless a real engine is configured); `ovl-sub::` `pkg/pi-subagents/experimental-subagents.test.ts`; `ovl-auth::` `pkg/pi-onboarding/provider-auth-provider.test.ts` |
| Observed / recorded evidence | `walk` = `llm-docs/pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/native-walkthrough.md` (guarded native GUI walkthrough); `pkg-ev` = `.../packaging.md`; `perf-ev` = `.../performance.md` |

"planned:" marks a test name that does not exist yet.

**Disposition** (including the owner-authorized scope amendment):

| Code | Plan term |
| --- | --- |
| `NE` | native equivalent |
| `NC` | native implementation of the same contract |
| `NP` | not present in the baseline (evidence cited in the row) |
| `PA` | pending audit |
| `BL` | blocked |
| `RC` | reserved for Codex — not implemented or qualified |
| `LB` | legacy bridge |
| `OS` | out of scope by explicit owner decision; excluded from release gates |

`NE`/`NC` here is the **target** disposition; it is not a pass. **Evidence** uses only:

- `measured` — a passing automated native test covers the row (or the part named); each row says
  "no differential" unless the test compares against reference-derived output (the fixture
  goldens, the Codex/tool reference captures, or import equivalence against
  `fixtures/pi-sessions/*/requests.jsonl`), in which case it says "differential vs reference
  fixture". "measured (part)" and "gap:" record what the cited tests do not cover;
- `observed` — seen in the guarded native walkthrough or a recorded packaging run (`walk`, `pkg-ev`);
- `failed` — a recorded run missed the row's assertion;
- `unverified` — code exists, no test;
- `not started` — no native implementation (with the reason, e.g. `NP`, `BL`, reserved).

Owner: **Opus** = main implementation; **Codex** = reserved final integration.

## Current status summary (2026-10-11)

WebSocket connection timeout and disabled-timeout cancellation now have real stalled-handshake
tests plus runtime settings forwarding. Native editor/terminal flags drive real launch effects
and recorder processes after a built-in write-tool change. Fixed built-in prompt sections retain
their frozen resources when source files become unreadable; the throwing-callback row is NE
for this supported registration, with arbitrary extension callbacks out of scope. Required
checks: **1534 passed, 0 failed, 53 ignored**, clean Clippy/formatting. All three remaining
explicitly unverified rows now have measured evidence. Partial measured-row gaps, reference
qualification, sustained/installed workflows and release gates remain open. See
[prompt/timeout/launcher evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/prompt-timeout-launch-checks.md).

Tool progress now checkpoints bounded output before publication. Interrupted and stopped
recovery preserves that output and truncation counts, settles tool state atomically, and
reaps orphaned tool processes. Early reopening no longer clears a storage failure while
the canceled execution is still unwinding. Required checks: **1529 passed, 0 failed,
53 ignored**, clean Clippy/formatting. OUT-RECOVERY moves unverified → measured; three
explicitly unverified rows remain. Full release qualification remains open. See
[recovery evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/tool-output-recovery.md).

Copilot login retries now have bounded/cancellable request and wait lifetimes plus safe
numeric-prefix/HTTP-date parsing. Parent usage views now include committed child model spend;
ancestor invalidation and child usage commit atomically. Saturated scheduling, replay/restart,
storage-failure recovery and rich imported totals have measured evidence. Required checks:
**1526 passed, 0 failed, 53 ignored**, clean Clippy/formatting. SUB-PERMITS and USE-TOTAL
move unverified → measured; four explicitly unverified rows remain. This is not full release
qualification. See [subagent evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/subagent-accounting.md).

Radius private catalog discovery and persistence now work for default/custom gateways,
including offline legacy migration, token rotation, local overrides, cancellation and restart.
A local gateway test completes a native write-tool turn and resumes after restart; six
executed reference sanitizer/converter cases agree. Required checks: **1518 passed, 0 failed,
53 ignored**, clean Clippy/formatting. PRV-radius returns PA → NC; full lifecycle/live/installed
qualification remains open. See [Radius evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/radius-catalog.md).

Native root/child removal is implemented with durable intent/recovery and desktop catch-up.
The prompt now identifies Pipkin and contains compiled product help. Eight executed prompt
reference cases preserve all other sections; provider-specific wire identities remain intact.
The remaining provider login reachability decisions preserve native service flows and the
existing two-provider desktop subscription UI. All eleven remaining PA rows move to NE;
this records implementation/equivalence decisions, not release qualification. Required
provider refresh checks, broader reliability/performance, installed workflows, packaging and
platform acceptance remain open. Native remains opt-in; no row is fully qualified.

## Prior status summary (history and reachability audit)

Native session rename, real compaction history paging and custom-shell settings now have
runtime/desktop integration coverage. P05-RENAME and API-key configuration move PA → NC;
custom shell PA → NE; faux-only polling and classic-only cache warming PA → NP. Custom

unsupported edited imports still refuse with their source preserved. This does not waive
ordinary history/context behavior. See [audit evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/history-reachability-audit.md).
Remote catalog refresh/offline behavior is confirmed required and still unimplemented.
Native remains opt-in; full qualification remains open.

Prior queued-summary batch:

Schema **8** adds durable passive writes for built-in manual/background summaries.
Busy summaries wait for a tool/final boundary, precede selected users, and survive Stop
and restart. Durable tool-boundary markers prevent skipping delivery or consuming an
extra steer after recovery. Equal cuts remain valid; only backward cuts are stale.
Queue capacity, migration, rollback and actual SIGKILL are measured. Executed pinned
inbox functions provide bounded placement/selection comparisons, not full scheduler
qualification. `Q-WRITES` moves PA → NC; see [queued-write evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/queued-summary-writes.md).
Native remains opt-in.

Prior compaction runtime batch:

Schema **7** activates durable manual/background/blocking/overflow compactions. Startup
and explicit reopen recover saved selections/responses/retries; usage, summary placement,
and terminal state commit atomically. Foreground Stop survives restart; background work
continues. Executed SIGKILL tests cover admission, selection, response storage, classification,
retry deadlines/budgets and overflow handoff. Bounded recovery and storage-failure tests
are recorded in [runtime evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/compaction-runtime.md).
`CTX-CHECKPOINT` moves PA → NC with measured runtime evidence; qualification/reference
comparison is still open. Native stays opt-in.

## 2. Coding tools and execution environment (P02)

Tool order the model sees on the durable path: `read, write, edit, bash, subagent`
(A-tools §1.0). Native: `E/tools::builtin_tools()` = `read, write, edit, bash`; the runtime appends
`subagent` for root sessions with subagents enabled (`E/runtime/mod.rs::tools_for`).

## 3. Wire adapters (P03)

All 10 baseline wire API kinds have native adapters in `E/provider/api/<kind>.rs` (anthropic-messages,
openai-completions, openai-responses, azure-openai-responses, openai-codex-responses,
google-generative-ai, google-vertex, mistral-conversations, bedrock-converse-stream,
pi-messages) plus Cloudflare request preparation; shared helpers live once in
`E/provider/api/common/` and `openai_responses_shared.rs`, plumbing in
`E/provider/{http,sse,errors,model,mock}.rs`. Adapter tests run against the local mock server
(`E/provider/mock.rs`). Only the Codex adapter's goldens (`openai_codex/fixtures/`, made by
running the reference `stream()` with an injected fetch) are reference captures; the other
adapters' expected requests/streams were written from the reference source ("no differential").
Claude Opus 5.5 has since passed live coding/lifecycle smoke checks (subscription evidence);
other provider rows still carry their local-fixture and unverified-live limits.

| ID | Source | Baseline behavior | Fixture / test | Owner · module | Migration mapping | Differential assertion | Coverage | Disp. | Evidence |

## 4. Provider ids (P03)

One row per provider id in A-prov §2 (42 built-in providers + `faux` = 43 ids). Each built-in
provider's pinned model data is embedded and hash-checked natively
(`mdl::embedded_files_match_the_pinned_manifest`, measured); request behavior depends on the
adapter rows above. Migration for every row: `auth.json` entry for that id → explicit import
(ADR 0007); `models.json` provider/model overrides for that id → explicit config import.
Differential for every row: golden request/stream for a text + tool turn against a mock server,
plus model list equality with the reference catalog (incl. `models.json` overrides).
Authorized live qualification is separate and owner-run. At `4fc4060` provider rows are measured
at the level of their API kind's adapter tests (plus provider-specific tests where cited); no
row has a provider-specific reference capture except `openai-codex`.

source, the difference must be reconciled by RE-01 before this section is called complete.

### 6.3 OAuth flows (one row per flow and lifecycle step)

The shipped onboarding exposes Claude and ChatGPT/Codex only. Native desktop retains that
allowlist; additional OAuth flows remain available through the native engine service as an
implemented advantage. D-07 is resolved under the active goal to retain native capabilities,
without adding desktop UI. Their stored credential use and refresh remain baseline-required.
Login rows are NE with local mock evidence; full provider differential/live qualification
is separate. See [reachability audit](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/identity-auth-audit.md).

is the differential target; Chord replicated-state op streams are not reproduced (ADR 0002).

No exact replay of old progress/auth; native frontend core events now have 128-event / 8 MiB capacity accounting through batch application, with one oversized event alone; a bounded Stop worker bypasses presentation waits and guards submission/connection ordering (`ne2e::stop_crosses_desktop_byte_pressure_and_settlement_recovers_after_reconnect`, `ne2e::shutdown_releases_a_native_worker_waiting_for_desktop_bytes`, `App/event_queue.rs` and `App/adapters/native/control.rs` tests); lifecycle/catalog and normal request admission remain separate; the provider generation channel is replaced by bounded synchronous commit batches (`pg::synchronous_bursts_are_bounded_and_unicode_and_tiny_deltas_remain_lossless`, `pg::checkpoint_failure_cancels_the_producer_and_stops_further_progress`, `rt::stop_interrupts_a_provider_that_keeps_emitting_without_yielding`); full message and transient input-unit limits remain separate; external attachment is implemented (ADR 0008); event fan-out now shares one bounded encoded body, with independent sequence/envelope/pressure (`dq::subscribers_share_one_payload_but_keep_independent_envelopes_and_pressure`, `dq::shared_envelopes_preserve_escaping_extreme_ids_and_write_errors`, `dq::escaped_payload_size_is_bounded_and_oversize_does_not_retain_the_event`, `delivery::shared_transport_preserves_reply_barriers_and_client_local_sequences`). Shared typed/JSON payload overhead is not a total heap budget. See delivery/memory, [shared delivery](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/shared-event-delivery.md) and [desktop pressure evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/desktop-pressure.md) |

| DUR-CORRUPT | docs/packaging.md "Damage" (desktop DB) | Corrupt engine data detection/recovery | st::a_damaged_database_is_refused_untouched_and_named; cp::a_damaged_database_stops_the_engine_at_start_and_is_left_untouched | Opus · E/store/schema.rs::check_existing | n/a | Refuse/recover per policy | — | NC | measured: st::a_damaged_database_is_refused_untouched_and_named (garbage file; a real database with damaged pages), cp::a_damaged_database_stops_the_engine_at_start_and_is_left_untouched (headless worker exits non-zero naming the file). Policy (E/store/schema.rs): before anything writes to an existing database it must read as SQLite and pass `PRAGMA quick_check`; otherwise the engine refuses to start with `corrupt engine data: <path> is damaged (…). It was left untouched; move it … or restore it from a backup`, and the file stays byte-identical (no WAL switch, migration, set-aside or repair; no `--recover`). Damage that surfaces later fails that session closed (DUR-FAILCLOSED). quick_check time grows with database size; startup cost on large profiles not measured |

## 13. Extensions and extension questions (P14, P15)

Baseline mechanism = Chord facet plugin packages loaded via `--pi-extension` → `pi server -e`
(A-ext §5.1–5.2). Arbitrary TypeScript facet compatibility needs Node; see ADR 0006. That stays
**out of scope by owner decision**. The native engine has its own optional versioned contract
(`E/extensions`, `docs/native-extensions.md`): in-process Rust extensions and JSON-lines process
extensions (`--engine-extension builtin:questions` or `--engine-extension <executable>`), a question host ported from
`ui-requests-provider.ts`, and native ports of `pi-example-questions` and `pi-example-plugin`.
Missing against the reference facet surface: hot reload, services published to
presentations, and general durable submission identities beyond input request keys; there is no sandbox.

| ID | Source | Baseline behavior | Fixture / test | Owner · module | Migration mapping | Differential assertion | Coverage | Disp. | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

## 15. Settings keys the durable harness reads (and server-level keys)

Durable harness reads only `stream` (provider retry/idle timeout), `compaction`, `retry`,
`steeringMode`, `followUpMode` and `skills` (A-prompt §7.4, `ce/durable/harness-setup.ts:25-49`,
`prompt.ts:34`). Server/model-runtime keys are listed separately. Native loader: `E/config.rs`
(`Settings`; unknown keys kept in `other`). Migration for every row: explicit import of
`~/.pi/agent/settings.json` and `<cwd>/.pi/settings.json` semantics (project file is read in
place, natively too).

## 16. Desktop workflows (P05, P06, P07, P16–P19)

### 16.1 Workflow tests in `App/adapters/pi/e2e.rs`

Each test is a reference workflow against the real Pi engine (ignored unless an engine is
configured). Target: parameterize the harness by backend (plan §8 "Dual-backend rollout") so the
same assertions run against the native backend. Owner for all rows: **Opus ·
`App/adapters/native`**. Migration for all rows: desktop DB/journal unchanged; conversation ↔
session id mapping by the importer. Native counterparts: 20 workflows were ported to
`App/adapters/native/e2e.rs` (`ne2e::`, run by default with the real core, NativeBackend, journal
and an in-process engine driven by a scripted provider); other rows cite the native unit tests
that cover them or say what is not ported. The Pi reference tests were not run for this ledger.

| ID | Test (e2e::) | Baseline behavior | Native fixture | Differential assertion | Coverage | Disp. | Evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

Final 500 turns grew 500 KiB, not the former ~27 MiB; allocator retention substantially reduced, not proof of zero leaks or a perfectly flat indefinite soak. Diagnostics and raw failed/intermediate measurements retained in delivery/memory evidence. Long-running-tool soak and non-glibc platforms remain unverified; historical perf-ev §2 failure is superseded only for the measured headless budget; fresh `4a9d4eb` run: 30,112 KiB idle / 41,588 KiB VmHWM, 13→13 fds, one process, +1.726 KiB/turn second-half slope; [evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/qualification-performance.md); mixed 720-turn / eight-session / two-active workload with 1 MiB real tool output and 64 KiB streams passes with three clean restarts: 30,444 KiB idle / 99,612 KiB VmHWM, 13 idle fds, zero settled descendants, 720 unique effects and byte-exact durable tool outputs. Per-epoch last-half RSS slopes +5.440/+3.598/+3.024 KiB/turn; residual-growth attribution and release qualification stay open ([mixed evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/mixed-workload.md)); follow-up [uninterrupted/desktop evidence](pipkin-rust-engine-evidence/desktop-soak.md): all 720 mixed turns in one worker lifetime pass at 31,380 KiB final RSS / 101,976 KiB VmHWM, +2.797 KiB/turn last-half slope. Actual 1,500-turn/four-session desktop verifies 300 real effects and durable outputs; server peak **131,140 KiB** misses 100 MiB target in the expanded single-active-turn workload, desktop final RSS 204,716 KiB with +12.511 KiB/turn last-half slope. Functional execution measured/visually observed; resource gate stays open |

## 19. Known reference defects (recorded, not silently fixed)

Each defect keeps the intended capability; any native correction needs its own regression test
and an entry under §20 or an owner decision under §21.

| ID | Where | Defect | Native handling |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| K-01 | ca/core/skills.ts:452-503; ce/durable/prompt.ts:34; A-prompt §2.4 | The durable server ignores `.agents/skills` (cwd/ancestors and `~/.agents/skills`) and package-installed skills, which the classic loader discovers; and it loads `<cwd>/.pi/skills` without the trust gate the classic loader applies | Reproduce the durable set for parity (PRM-SKILLS-DISCOVERY). Adding `.agents/skills` is an owner decision, not a silent fix. At `4fc4060`: reproduced (res::discovery_order_is_config_then_project_then_settings_paths_and_first_name_wins) |
| K-02 | ca/core/tools/bash.ts:45-48,194-208; A-tools §1.4 | The rules section tells the model "You can inspect PI_* environment variables for current model and session details", but the durable bash tool never injects `PI_*` variables (`exposeSessionEnvironment` unset) | Record; decide whether to drop the bullet (prompt change) or inject variables (env change). Either is a behavior change needing a test. At `4fc4060`: reproduced (bullet kept in `E/context/prompt.rs`, no `PI_*` variables injected) |
| K-03 | env/node.ts:776-790,1194-1218 | Bash full-output spill files in `$TMPDIR` are never deleted; unbounded temp growth | Native retains output in SQLite with a quota (I-04); spill paths in imported history stay as text. At `4fc4060` the native bash tool also still writes a private spill file (`Full output: <path>`) with no cleanup — decision D-10 |
| K-04 | D/harness/submissions.ts:160-219 | Same request key with different content silently returns the original submission | Native returns `Conflict` (I-01) |
| K-05 | ce/plugins/package.ts:27-28; App/adapters/pi/engine.rs:183-185 | Plugin selection is sticky: omitting `--pi-extension` restores the last list and Pipkin never sends an empty list, so a removed extension keeps loading | Record; native selection must be explicit and clearable; import reports the sticky profile. At `4fc4060`: native extensions are explicit per launch (`--engine-extension`); the importer did not read plugin profiles at that revision. Current import preserves profiles as inactive source bytes without blocking history solely on selections (IMP-PLUGIN-STATE); extension compatibility is OS |
| K-06 | ca/core/model-config.ts (schema) vs types; A-prov §5 | `chatTemplateKwargs` `$var` schema allows only `thinking.enabled` and `thinking.effort` while types also allow `thinking.budget` | Accept the schema's set; record |
| K-07 | A-tools §8.3 | The server path performs no image size/count/MIME validation; images stored verbatim as base64 inside entry JSON | Native enforces explicit limits (D-03) |
| K-08 | svc/subagents-provider.ts:101-145 | Every `view/open/page` rescans the whole child list; 64 live mounts per worker shared by all clients, never released before worker retirement | Native pages indexed `subagent_tasks`; no mount cap (I-06) |
| K-09 | packaging/pi-subagents/README.md:17; tool.ts:163-164 | Subagent recovery contract is metadata-only (not a code signature); identical-contract code changes are not proven safe | Native ports the contract check (SUB-RECOVERY-CONTRACT); provenance proposal I-05 |
| K-10 | durable/README.md:563 | `synchronous=NORMAL`: newest commit may be lost on power failure | Native `FULL` (I-02); power loss still not claimed |
| K-11 | docs/extensions.md | Says the contract lives in `../pi-fork/pi` and calls `--pi-extension` packages extensions; shipped engine is the pinned archive + overlays and loads facet packages (A-ext §5.6) | Documentation fix outside this ledger |

## 20. Intentional improvements (each needs its own regression test)

| ID | Improvement | Row(s) | Test |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| I-01 | Same key + different payload → `Conflict` instead of silently returning the original | ADM-KEY | st::same_key_same_payload_is_the_original_and_changed_payload_conflicts (measured) |
| I-02 | `synchronous=FULL` instead of `NORMAL` | DUR-PRAGMAS | st::acknowledged_writes_survive_reopen, wp::admission_survives_sigkill_and_is_found_by_lookup_after_restart (measured; not a power-loss proof) |
| I-03 | Stop retirement via an entry `retired` flag instead of `pi.cancelled-input` omit-edit entries (same observable context) | STOP-RETIRE | rt::stop_settles_aborted_and_retires_the_instruction_from_the_next_context, ne2e::stopping_a_prompt_flushes_its_instruction_before_a_new_prompt (measured); imported omit edits → retired: rti::reference_fixtures_import_open_and_continue_equivalently (`c-stop-then-prompt`) |
| I-04 | Retained tool output stored in SQLite (`tool_outputs`, ≤16 MiB per call) with honest dropped-byte counts, instead of never-cleaned temp files | OUT-RETAINED, TOOL-BASH-SPILL | st::tool_output_is_retained_with_honest_drop_counts, rt::real_tools_run_their_effects_are_recorded_and_the_complete_output_is_retained (measured). Note: the native bash tool still also writes a private spill file (TOOL-BASH-SPILL) |
| I-05 | Engine provenance with the subagent recovery contract (proposed) | SUB-RECOVERY-CONTRACT | contract recorded per call in kv `tool_contract:<call>` and compared before replay (run.rs); mismatch tested: rtf::a_changed_subagent_contract_gives_no_recovery_authority; provenance part needs owner sign-off |
| I-06 | Child history paged from indexed tasks; no 64-mount cap; inspector pages beyond 100-row/80-entry bounds | SUB-LIVE, SUB-PAGE, SUB-UI-* | nt::child_history_pages_by_cursor_and_the_selected_child_refreshes_live, ui-sub::five_thousand_child_entries_page_in_above_the_reading_position, ui-sub::a_thousand_tasks_mount_a_bounded_window_and_every_task_is_reachable (measured) |
| I-07 | One owned engine child, no global server discovery or process sweep | SVC-PROC-WORKERS, E2E-01/06 | nsup:: (7 tests), ew::the_worker_answers_over_its_pipes_and_exits_when_its_input_closes (measured); walk §4 (observed) |
| I-08 | Explicit byte/count limits on inputs, frames, queue (refusal, never truncation) | ADM-BOUNDS, Q-BOUND, XPORT-FRAME | rt::inputs_are_bounded_before_anything_is_recorded; frm:: tests; st::queue_entries_are_ordered_withdrawable_and_bounded (measured) |
| I-09 | `--data-dir` isolates engine state too | FLAG-DATA-DIR | ctl::the_native_namespace_names_the_engine_data_directory (measured); walk (observed) |
| I-10 | `CreateSession` idempotent under a request key | SVC-MGMT-CREATE | st::create_is_idempotent_under_its_key (measured) |
| I-11 | Typed successful-mutation events for Changes instead of provider-shaped JSON inference | DSK-CHANGES | nmap::changed_files_are_successful_session_edits_after_the_last_clean_scan, ne2e::a_session_edit_shows_in_changes_until_it_is_committed (measured) |
| I-12 | Indexed head-marker and open-run lookups: opening a session does not scan its history | SVC-HISTORY-PAGE, PLAT-PERF | perf::history after fixes: OpenSession p50 0.69/0.92/0.65 ms at 1k/10k/100k entries (measured, perf-ev "After fixes") |
| I-13 | Streaming commits within 40 ms of the oldest pending delta (durability unchanged) | GEN-02, PLAT-PERF | perf::stream_latency provider-progress run: p95 25.860–45.092 ms, no missing markers; headless, not display latency |
